- **politica-esbrasil**: Política da ES Brasil: uma matéria por hora (dias úteis 7h–20h; fim de semana e feriado de 2 em 2 horas), só agenda positiva da política capixaba — Governo, Ales, prefeituras, bancada, TJES e TCE-ES —, parceiros primeiro. - **poll-question-writer**: Use when the user asks for live poll questions, audience voting questions, slide poll content, workshop or meeting poll ideas, opinion/temperature/prediction questions, or wants an existing poll question improved. Turns slide content, a topic, or a presentation objective into strong poll questions with useful answer options — no leading questions, no weak distractors, no obvious answers, no generic wording. ([sambessey/poll-question-writer](https://github.com/sambessey/poll-question-writer/tree/HEAD/skills/poll-question-writer))
- **poll-slack**: Narrate new Slack messages in a channel or thread since a cursor. Returns a natural-language story of what was said, or a clear statement when there is nothing new. Use when a parent agent polls Slack and needs to know what changed, read a Slack delta, or understand a thread update without re-ingesting the whole thread. ([doodledood/manifest-dev](https://github.com/doodledood/manifest-dev/tree/HEAD/claude-plugins/manifest-dev/skills/poll-slack) · ★79 · 2 copies)
- **poll-slack**: Poll Slack channels for unanswered questions directed at the user. Use this skill when checking Slack for new questions to answer, or during an engineer-agent poll cycle. ([tomharris/engineer-agent](https://github.com/tomharris/engineer-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/poll-slack))
- **poll-slite**: Poll Slite for design documents tagged for review. Use this skill when checking Slite for docs to review, or during an engineer-agent poll cycle. ([tomharris/engineer-agent](https://github.com/tomharris/engineer-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/poll-slite))
- **poll-trigger-registration**: Register Connector Namespace trigger configs for Azure Functions host-pull Poll delivery. USE WHEN: creating deliveryMode Poll trigger configs, obtaining pollingEndpoints, deriving &lt;Connection&gt;__pollingEndpoint and TriggerConfigName settings, configuring Poll identity access, enabling Poll triggers, or troubleshooting Poll registration. NOT FOR: callback/Webhook notificationDetails (use webhook-trigger-registration), connection creation or OAuth consent (use connection-setup), or extension internals. ([Azure/azure-functions-connector-extension](https://github.com/Azure/azure-functions-connector-extension/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/poll-trigger-registration) · ★1)
- **poll-until**: Base primitive — bounded polling engine that repeats any check command until it succeeds or attempts are exhausted, emitting per-attempt JSONL plus a final verdict. Domain-agnostic; composed by GitHub API pollers, email waiters, and repo-transfer completion polls. ([baneeishaque/ai-suite](https://github.com/baneeishaque/ai-suite/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/general/polling/poll-until))
- **poll-worker-launch**: Launch background poll workers for parallel jobs. ([NikolaRHristov/STE-Code](https://github.com/NikolaRHristov/STE-Code/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ste-code-dev/poll-worker-launch) · ★19)
- **pollar**: **Pollar** is the onboarding-to-payment infrastructure layer for consumer apps on Stellar. The full stack from social login to USDC payments — without exposing users to blockchain complexity. ([Trustless-Work/cminds-coastal](https://github.com/Trustless-Work/cminds-coastal/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/pollar) · 2 copies)
- **pollar-upgrade**: Upgrade an app that uses the Pollar SDK (@pollar/core, @pollar/react, @pollar/react-native) from any release between 0.5 and 0.11 to 0.12, the release on SDK API v3. Runs the @pollar/migrate codemod, makes the changes it leaves by hand, and verifies the build. Use when an app is on an old @pollar release, when SDK API v1 answers 410 or sends a Deprecation header, or when the user asks to update Pollar. ([pollar-xyz/pollar](https://github.com/pollar-xyz/pollar/tree/HEAD/skills/pollar-upgrade) · ★12)
- **pollar-wallet-auth**: Add embedded Stellar wallets and local fiat on/off-ramps to a web or React Native app with @pollar/core and @pollar/react. Users sign in with email, social, or a passkey and get a funded account with no seed phrase; the same client quotes and runs fiat deposits and withdrawals, payments, sponsored (gasless) activation and trustlines, multi-venue swaps, and SEP-53 / SEP-10 ownership proofs. Use when an app needs Stellar wallets for people who do not have one, or needs to move money between fiat and Stellar. ([pollar-xyz/pollar](https://github.com/pollar-xyz/pollar/tree/HEAD/skills/pollar-wallet-auth) · ★12)
- **pollard-doctor**: Diagnoses a broken pollard install — MCP server unreachable, missing binaries, profile load failures, unsymbolicated frames. Use when any pollard tool call fails, when load_profile errors out, or when the user reports "the profiler isn't working." ([antiguru/pollard](https://github.com/antiguru/pollard/tree/HEAD/skills/pollard-doctor))
- **pollard-weights**: Quantize any LLM the RIGHT way with Pollard Weights and ship it on every lane: GGUF (full ladder + trellis flagship), MLX, GPTQ, MX (NVFP4 / MXFP4 / FP8 / MXFP6 + mixes), EXL3, EXLR8 and DRAFT (speculative drafters), one repo per model. Use this whenever asked to quantize / shrink / "make a Pollard build" / "pollard it" / release a model, or to plan its fit on a machine or wafer. It routes dense vs MoE models down different paths — using the wrong one wastes hours. ([WestWaters/pollard-weights](https://github.com/WestWaters/pollard-weights/tree/HEAD/skills/pollard) · ★34)
- **pollclaw**: Doodle for Agents and Humans. Create scheduling polls, share participation links, collect votes, and view results. - **pollen**: How to install, upgrade and use pollen to deploy and pin Agent Skills (pollen.yaml → .claude/skills and .agents/skills). - **pollen**: Pollen & hay fever skill. Fetches real-time pollen + weather for any city, tracks attack history with symptom + medication logging, advises on window open/close times, computes cumulative 72h exposure, flags outdoor calendar events, and correlates attack patterns with wearable HRV/resting heart rate data.
